Not like every time, and I don’t want to make it sound like I’m totally out of control, but once I start with takeout or snacks late at night, I can kind of keep going past the point I meant to stop. Then other days I barely remember to eat, so it’s just been really all over the place.\n\nDoctor: Do these late-night eating episodes upset you a lot?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I mean, I try not to make a huge deal out of it, but it does bother me. It’s not every single night. But it’s enough that I noticed, like, some days I’ll basically forget meals, and then other nights after midnight I’m just going through takeout and snacks. That pattern is what kind of scared me, honestly.\n\nDoctor: On average, do these late-night eating episodes happen at least once a week?\n\nPatient: Yeah… I think probably at least once a week, give or take. I haven’t tracked it or anything, but the thing that scared me is my eating’s been all over the place—some days I’ll forget meals completely, and then other nights I’m up after midnight going through takeout and snacks.     "psychosocial_stressor": true,
    "additional_requirements": [
      "Passive suicidal ideation acknowledged without plan or intent",
      "Work-related stressor/trouble at work preceded or accompanied worsening mood",
      "No evidence of being underweight",
      "Night eating was not due to awakening from sleep"
    ]
  }
}
